  DOCUMENT SAYS:
    m:137 "**Why fixed (B11 with its condition-(4) clause):** under nesting a higher rate is at

    upper bound, fails at every higher rung if it fails at the lowest"
  RECORD SAYS:
    Code's B11 fix (session transcript 3f58e88e…jsonl, 2026-09-27T22:28Z; also quoted in the

  CHECKER NOTE:
    NOT RESOLVED. The clause v1.8 labels as B11's says the opposite of B11's exception. It asserts
